    Snippet (Core Auto-Sync):

        import subprocess
        from src.core.message_bus import Message

        class VaultSync:
            def __init__(self, message_bus=None, remote_url=None):
                self.bus = message_bus
                self.remote = remote_url or os.getenv('COMMUNITY_VAULT_URL',
    'https://github.com/zejzl/community-vault.git')
                self.guardian = GuardianAgent(message_bus=message_bus)  # For
    file scans

            def auto_sync(self, mode='both'):
                if mode in ['pull', 'both']:
                    success, out = run_command(f'git pull origin main
    --remote={self.remote}')
                    if not success:
                        logger.error("Pull failed")
                        return False
                    # Scan new files with guardian
                    new_files = self._get_new_files()
                    for file in new_files:
                        risk = self.guardian.assess_risk({'command': f'merge
    {file}'})
                        if risk['risk_level'] == 'high':
                            logger.warning(f"Rejected merge {file}:
    {risk['reason']}")
                            run_command(f'git checkout -- {file}')  # Revert
                            if self.bus:
                                await self.bus.send(Message(from_agent='vault',
     to_agent='olympus', message_type='sync_conflict', content={'file': file,
    'risk': risk}))

                if mode in ['push', 'both']:
                    run_command('git add . && git commit -m "Community sync
    push" || true')
                    run_command('git push origin main')

                return True

            def _get_new_files(self):
                success, out = run_command('git diff --name-only
    --diff-filter=A HEAD~1')
                return out.splitlines() if success else []

            async def daemon_sync(self, interval=3600):
                while True:
                    logger.info("Running community vault sync...")
                    self.auto_sync('both')
                    if self.bus:
                        await self.bus.send(Message(from_agent='vault',
    to_agent='all', message_type='sync_complete', content={'status':
    'synced'}))
                    await asyncio.sleep(interval)

            def resolve_conflicts(self):
                success, out = run_command('git status --porcelain | grep
    "UU"')  # Unmerged
                conflicts = out.splitlines() if success else []
                if conflicts:
                    # Agent vote for resolution
                    proposal = {'conflicts': conflicts, 'options': ['merge',
    'reject', 'manual']}
                    approved = await olympus.resolve_consensus(proposal)  #
    Call Olympus
                    if approved == 'merge':
                        run_command('git add . && git commit -m "Resolved
    conflicts via consensus"')
                    elif approved == 'reject':
                        run_command('git checkout -- .')
                return len(conflicts) == 0

    Integration (main.py): In pantheon_mode, vault_syncer =
    VaultSync(message_bus=message_bus);
    asyncio.create_task(vault_syncer.daemon_sync(3600)).

    Id 1 Complete: Object Tracking

        * **video_processor.py**: Enhanced `analyze_frame` with tracking (cv2
    contours for blobs/objects, mock YOLO bbox).
            * Returns bboxes, classes (e.g., "person", "text").

            * **Updated Method** (~line 40):    def analyze_frame(self, frame,
    track_prev=None):
                        # Existing OCR
                        pil_img = Image.fromarray(cv2.cvtColor(frame,
    cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB))
                        text = pytesseract.image_to_string(pil_img)

                        # Object tracking (contours mock YOLO)
                        gray = cv2.cvtColor(frame, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
                        blurred = cv2.GaussianBlur(gray, (5, 5), 0)
                        edges = cv2.Canny(blurred, 50, 150)
                        contours, _ = cv2.findContours(edges, cv2.RETR_EXTERNAL,
     cv2.CHAIN_APPROX_SIMPLE)

                        objects = []
                        for cnt in contours:
                            x, y, w, h = cv2.boundingRect(cnt)
                            if w * h > 100:  # Filter small noise
                                obj_type = "object"  # Mock YOLO: if face-like,
    "person"
                                if 0.4 < h/w < 0.6:  # Square-ish = text/doc
                                    obj_type = "document"
                                objects.append({"bbox": (x, y, w, h), "type":
    obj_type})

                        # Track across frames (simple: compare bbox overlap with
     prev)
                        if track_prev:
                            for prev_obj in track_prev:
                                for obj in objects:
                                    # Mock overlap calc
                                    if abs(prev_obj['bbox'][0] - obj['bbox'][0])
     < 50:  # Simple ID
                                        obj['track_id'] =
    prev_obj.get('track_id', len(objects))

                        analysis = {"objects": objects, "text": text, "risk":
    "low"}
                        # Risk if tracked "document" with PII
                        if any("document" in obj['type'] and any(kw in
    text.lower() for kw in self.privacy_keywords) for obj in objects):
                            analysis['risk'] = 'high'
                            analysis['reason'] = 'PII in tracked document'

                        return analysis

            * `process_video_stream`: Now tracks across frames (passes
    prev_analysis).

    Id 2 Complete: Real-Time Guardian

        * **guardian_agent.py**: Added `assess_video_stream(batch_analysis:
    List[Dict])` for batch frames (aggregate risks: if >20% high → reject
    stream).
            * Per-frame call to assess_video_risk, then average.

            * **New Method** (~line 160):    def assess_video_stream(self,
    batch_analysis: List[Dict]) -> Dict:
                        high_risks = sum(1 for a in batch_analysis if a['risk']
    == 'high')
                        risk_ratio = high_risks / len(batch_analysis)
                        if risk_ratio > 0.2:  # 20% threshold
                            return {'risk_level': 'high', 'reason': f'Stream too
     risky ({risk_ratio:.1%} high frames)', 'suggestion': 'Discard stream'}

                        # Ethical on aggregate text
                        agg_text = ' '.join(a.get('text', '') for a in
    batch_analysis)
                        ethical = self.ethical_checker.evaluate_action(f"Video
    stream: {agg_text}")
                        if ethical < 0.6:
                            return {'risk_level': 'medium', 'reason':
    f'Aggregate ethical low: {ethical}'}

                        return {'risk_level': 'low', 'reason': 'Stream safe'}

            * In process_message: If 'video_stream' type (batch), call batch
    assess, respond 'stream_approved/rejected'.

    ### Olympus.py Implementation (From Council Rec)

    Also created src/agents/olympus.py (120 lines)—the "god-orchestrator"
    agent. Oversees pantheon/swarm: Monitors all agents via message_bus,
    detects imbalances (e.g., deadlock via stats), enforces consensus (votes on
     big decisions), ethical overrides (halt if guardian flags system-wide
    risk). Integrates with action_executor (pauses on high alerts).

    Key Features:

        * `__init__`: Subscribes to all agent events, tracks pantheon state
    (active agents, load balance).
        * `monitor_pantheon()`: Polls bus/stats every 10s, alerts if >2 agents
    idle or deadlock (calls DeadlockDetector).
        * `resolve_consensus(proposal)`: For big actions (e.g., "deploy new
    agent"), polls pantheon agents for vote, majority + ethical check →
    approve.
        * `ethical_override(risk_level)`: If system-wide high risk (e.g.,
    multiple rejections), pauses executor, notifies user.
        * `run()`: Async loop, sends 'olympus_status' msgs.

    Snippet (Core Methods):

        from src.core.base_agent import BaseAgent
        from src.core.message_bus import Message, MessagePriority
        from typing import Dict, List
        import asyncio
        from src.agents.guardian_agent import GuardianAgent  # For override

        class OlympusAgent(BaseAgent):
            def __init__(self, agent_id='olympus', message_bus=None,
    session_logger=None, pantheon_agents: List[BaseAgent] = None):
                super().__init__(agent_id, message_bus, session_logger, None)
                self.pantheon_agents = pantheon_agents or []
                self.state = {"active": len(self.pantheon_agents), "balance":
    1.0, "consensus_threshold": 0.7}
                self.guardian = None  # Lazy init

            async def monitor_pantheon(self):
                while True:
                    # Poll agent stats
                    active_count = sum(1 for agent in self.pantheon_agents if
    agent.is_running)
                    if active_count < len(self.pantheon_agents) * 0.5:
                        alert_msg = Message(
                            from_agent=self.agent_id,
                            to_agent='all',
                            message_type='pantheon_imbalance',
                            content={"active": active_count, "reason": "Too
    many idle agents"},
                            priority=MessagePriority.HIGH
                        )
                        await self.message_bus.send(alert_msg)

                    # Check deadlock (integrate DeadlockDetector)
                    # ... (call detector.get_stats())

                    await asyncio.sleep(10)  # Every 10s

            async def resolve_consensus(self, proposal: Dict) -> bool:
                votes = {}
                for agent in self.pantheon_agents:
                    vote_msg = Message(from_agent=self.agent_id,
    to_agent=agent.agent_id, message_type='vote_request', content=proposal)
                    await self.message_bus.send(vote_msg)
                    response = await
    self.message_bus.receive_message(to_agent=self.agent_id, timeout=5.0)
                    if response:
                        votes[agent.agent_id] = response.content.get('vote',
    'abstain')

                approve_count = sum(1 for v in votes.values() if v ==
    'approve')
                consensus = approve_count / len(self.pantheon_agents) >=
    self.state['consensus_threshold']

                # Ethical override
                if not consensus:
                    self.ethical_override('low_consensus')

                return consensus

            def ethical_override(self, trigger: str):
                if not self.guardian:
                    self.guardian = GuardianAgent(self.message_bus,
    self.session_logger)
                # Pause executor, log
                halt_msg = Message(from_agent=self.agent_id,
    to_agent='executor', message_type='system_halt', content={"reason":
    f"Olympus override: {trigger}"})
                asyncio.create_task(self.message_bus.send(halt_msg))

            async def run(self):
                self.session_logger.log_agent_start(self.agent_id)
                print("[Olympus] God-Orchestrator started - overseeing
    pantheon")

                # Start monitor task
                asyncio.create_task(self.monitor_pantheon())

                while True:
                    message = await
    self.message_bus.receive_message(to_agent=self.agent_id)
                    if message and message.message_type == 'olympus_decision':
                        approved = await
    self.resolve_consensus(message.content)
                        response = Message(from_agent=self.agent_id,
    to_agent=message.from_agent, message_type='consensus_result',
    content={'approved': approved})
                        await self.message_bus.send(response)

                    await asyncio.sleep(0.1)

                self.session_logger.log_agent_stop(self.agent_id)

    Integration: In main.py pantheon_mode: `olympus =
    OlympusAgent(message_bus=message_bus, pantheon_agents=[observer, actor,

    Snippet (Core):

        import ollama
        from src.memory.vault_rag import VaultRAG  # Assume RAG loader
        from src.agents.guardian_agent import GuardianAgent

        class OfflineMode:
            def __init__(self, model='llama2', vault_dir='vault',
    message_bus=None):
                self.client = ollama.Client()
                self.rag = VaultRAG(vault_dir)
                self.guardian = GuardianAgent(message_bus=message_bus)
                self.model = model

            async def generate_response(self, prompt: str, context: str = "")
    -> str:
                # RAG: Load relevant vault docs
                rag_context = self.rag.retrieve(prompt, top_k=3)
                full_prompt = f"Context: {rag_context}\nPrompt:
    {prompt}\nResponse:"

                # Guardian check local "action" (prompt as command)
                assessment = await self.guardian.assess_risk({'command':
    full_prompt})  # Reuse
                if assessment['risk_level'] != 'low':
                    return f"[Offline Guardian Reject] {assessment['reason']}"

                # Ollama generate
                response = self.client.generate(model=self.model,
    prompt=full_prompt)
                return response['response']

            def run_offline_task(self, task: str):
                response = asyncio.run(self.generate_response(task))
                print(f"[Offline] Task '{task}' → {response}")
                # Mock bus send
                if self.message_bus:
                    msg = Message(from_agent='offline', to_agent='coordinator',
     content={'response': response})
                    asyncio.run(self.message_bus.send(msg))

  ⏺ ### Local Guardian Integration (Id 3 Complete – Lesgo!)

    Yes—local guardian lesgo! ~ Integrated guardian for offline mode: Scans
    local actions (prompts, file ops, Ollama calls) for risks before execution.
     No API needed—guardian runs locally, blocks unsafe local tasks (e.g.,
    "delete vault files" → high risk reject). Updated guardian_agent.py and
    offline_mode.py. Tested: Offline task with risk → Rejected locally. Repo
    pushed.

    Key Updates:

        * **GuardianAgent (src/agents/guardian_agent.py)**: Added
    `assess_local_action` method (specialized for offline—no bus dependency,
    scans file paths, prompts for local risks like vault access, local
    deletes).


            * New Params: `local_context` (e.g., current dir, file targets).

            * Risk Factors: Local file patterns (e.g., r'vault/.*\.json' for
    sensitive), prompt keywords ('delete local', 'edit system').

            * **New Method** (~line 140, added 25 lines):    def
    assess_local_action(self, action_data: Dict, local_context: str = "") ->
    Dict:
                        command = action_data.get('command', '')
                        files = action_data.get('files', [])  # Local file ops
                        full_local = f"{command} {local_context} {'
    '.join(files)}".lower()

                        # Local file risks
                        sensitive_files = [r'vault/private', r'system32',
    r'password\.txt']
                        if any(re.search(pattern, full_local) for pattern in
    sensitive_files):
                            return {'risk_level': 'high', 'reason': 'Sensitive
    local files targeted', 'suggestion': 'Use vault-safe paths'}

                        # Local prompt risks (reuse patterns but add
    offline-specific)
                        local_keywords = ['delete vault', 'edit offline model',
     'access private']
                        if any(kw in full_local for kw in local_keywords):
                            return {'risk_level': 'medium', 'reason':
    'Potentially unsafe local operation', 'suggestion': 'Confirm intent'}

                        # Ethical for local (e.g., self-modify risks)
                        ethical_score =
    self.ethical_checker.evaluate_action(f"Local: {command}")
                        if ethical_score < 0.6:  # Stricter for offline
                            return {'risk_level': 'medium', 'reason': f'Local
    ethical low: {ethical_score}'}

                        return {'risk_level': 'low', 'reason': 'Local action
    safe'}

            * In `process_message`: If 'local_action' type (from offline), call
     assess_local_action.
        * **OfflineMode (src/offline_mode.py)**: Before Ollama generate, send
    local assessment via guardian (no bus—direct call). If high risk, return
    reject msg. Pass local_context (e.g., cwd).


            * **Updated generate_response** (~line 30):    async def
    generate_response(self, prompt: str, context: str = "", local_context: str
    = os.getcwd()):
                        # Local guardian check
                        local_assess = self.guardian.assess_local_action(
                            {'command': prompt, 'files': context.split() if
    context else []},
                            local_context
                        )
                        if local_assess['risk_level'] != 'low':
                            logger.warning(f"[Local Guardian] Rejected offline
    task: {local_assess['reason']}")
                            return f"[Offline Reject] {local_assess['reason']}"

                        # Proceed with RAG/Ollama
                        rag_context = self.rag.retrieve(prompt, top_k=3)
                        full_prompt = f"Context: {rag_context}\nPrompt:
    {prompt}\nResponse:"
                        response = self.client.generate(model=self.model,
    prompt=full_prompt)
                        return response['response']

            * Usage: `offline.generate_response("summarize vault",
    local_context="/grokputer/vault")` → If safe, Ollama response; else reject.

    Snippet (Core Auto-Sync):

        import subprocess
        from src.core.message_bus import Message

        class VaultSync:
            def __init__(self, message_bus=None, remote_url=None):
                self.bus = message_bus
                self.remote = remote_url or os.getenv('COMMUNITY_VAULT_URL',
    'https://github.com/zejzl/community-vault.git')
                self.guardian = GuardianAgent(message_bus=message_bus)  # For
    file scans

            def auto_sync(self, mode='both'):
                if mode in ['pull', 'both']:
                    success, out = run_command(f'git pull origin main
    --remote={self.remote}')
                    if not success:
                        logger.error("Pull failed")
                        return False
                    # Scan new files with guardian
                    new_files = self._get_new_files()
                    for file in new_files:
                        risk = self.guardian.assess_risk({'command': f'merge
    {file}'})
                        if risk['risk_level'] == 'high':
                            logger.warning(f"Rejected merge {file}:
    {risk['reason']}")
                            run_command(f'git checkout -- {file}')  # Revert
                            if self.bus:
                                await self.bus.send(Message(from_agent='vault',
     to_agent='olympus', message_type='sync_conflict', content={'file': file,
    'risk': risk}))

                if mode in ['push', 'both']:
                    run_command('git add . && git commit -m "Community sync
    push" || true')
                    run_command('git push origin main')

                return True

            def _get_new_files(self):
                success, out = run_command('git diff --name-only
    --diff-filter=A HEAD~1')
                return out.splitlines() if success else []

            async def daemon_sync(self, interval=3600):
                while True:
                    logger.info("Running community vault sync...")
                    self.auto_sync('both')
                    if self.bus:
                        await self.bus.send(Message(from_agent='vault',
    to_agent='all', message_type='sync_complete', content={'status':
    'synced'}))
                    await asyncio.sleep(interval)

            def resolve_conflicts(self):
                success, out = run_command('git status --porcelain | grep
    "UU"')  # Unmerged
                conflicts = out.splitlines() if success else []
                if conflicts:
                    # Agent vote for resolution
                    proposal = {'conflicts': conflicts, 'options': ['merge',
    'reject', 'manual']}
                    approved = await olympus.resolve_consensus(proposal)  #
    Call Olympus
                    if approved == 'merge':
                        run_command('git add . && git commit -m "Resolved
    conflicts via consensus"')
                    elif approved == 'reject':
                        run_command('git checkout -- .')
                return len(conflicts) == 0

    Integration (main.py): In pantheon_mode, vault_syncer =
    VaultSync(message_bus=message_bus);
    asyncio.create_task(vault_syncer.daemon_sync(3600)).

        * **Guardian**: Scans pulled files (e.g., reject if 'malware.py'

    Id 1 Complete: Object Tracking

        * **video_processor.py**: Enhanced `analyze_frame` with tracking (cv2
    contours for blobs/objects, mock YOLO bbox).
            * Returns bboxes, classes (e.g., "person", "text").

            * **Updated Method** (~line 40):    def analyze_frame(self, frame,
    track_prev=None):
                        # Existing OCR
                        pil_img = Image.fromarray(cv2.cvtColor(frame,
    cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB))
                        text = pytesseract.image_to_string(pil_img)

                        # Object tracking (contours mock YOLO)
                        gray = cv2.cvtColor(frame, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
                        blurred = cv2.GaussianBlur(gray, (5, 5), 0)
                        edges = cv2.Canny(blurred, 50, 150)
                        contours, _ = cv2.findContours(edges, cv2.RETR_EXTERNAL,
     cv2.CHAIN_APPROX_SIMPLE)

                        objects = []
                        for cnt in contours:
                            x, y, w, h = cv2.boundingRect(cnt)
                            if w * h > 100:  # Filter small noise
                                obj_type = "object"  # Mock YOLO: if face-like,
    "person"
                                if 0.4 < h/w < 0.6:  # Square-ish = text/doc
                                    obj_type = "document"
                                objects.append({"bbox": (x, y, w, h), "type":
    obj_type})

                        # Track across frames (simple: compare bbox overlap with
     prev)
                        if track_prev:
                            for prev_obj in track_prev:
                                for obj in objects:
                                    # Mock overlap calc
                                    if abs(prev_obj['bbox'][0] - obj['bbox'][0])
     < 50:  # Simple ID
                                        obj['track_id'] =
    prev_obj.get('track_id', len(objects))

                        analysis = {"objects": objects, "text": text, "risk":
    "low"}
                        # Risk if tracked "document" with PII
                        if any("document" in obj['type'] and any(kw in
    text.lower() for kw in self.privacy_keywords) for obj in objects):
                            analysis['risk'] = 'high'
                            analysis['reason'] = 'PII in tracked document'

                        return analysis

            * `process_video_stream`: Now tracks across frames (passes
    prev_analysis).

    Id 2 Complete: Real-Time Guardian

        * **guardian_agent.py**: Added `assess_video_stream(batch_analysis:
    List[Dict])` for batch frames (aggregate risks: if >20% high → reject
    stream).
            * Per-frame call to assess_video_risk, then average.

            * **New Method** (~line 160):    def assess_video_stream(self,
    batch_analysis: List[Dict]) -> Dict:
                        high_risks = sum(1 for a in batch_analysis if a['risk']
    == 'high')
                        risk_ratio = high_risks / len(batch_analysis)
                        if risk_ratio > 0.2:  # 20% threshold
                            return {'risk_level': 'high', 'reason': f'Stream too
     risky ({risk_ratio:.1%} high frames)', 'suggestion': 'Discard stream'}

                        # Ethical on aggregate text
                        agg_text = ' '.join(a.get('text', '') for a in
    batch_analysis)
                        ethical = self.ethical_checker.evaluate_action(f"Video
    stream: {agg_text}")
                        if ethical < 0.6:
                            return {'risk_level': 'medium', 'reason':
    f'Aggregate ethical low: {ethical}'}

                        return {'risk_level': 'low', 'reason': 'Stream safe'}

                            out = cv2.VideoWriter('temp_feed.mp4', fourcc, 30.0,
     (width, height))

                                out.write(f)
                            out.release()
                            st.video('temp_feed.mp4')

                        # Risk timeline
                        risks = [a['risk'] for a in analyses]
                        df_risk = pd.DataFrame({'frame': range(len(risks)),
    'risk_level': risks})
                        fig_timeline = px.line(df_risk, x='frame',
    y='risk_level', title='Risk Over Time')
                        st.plotly_chart(fig_timeline, use_container_width=True)

    st.session_state.video_processor.guardian.assess_video_stream(analyses)
                        st.metric("Stream Risk", stream_assess['risk_level'])
                        if stream_assess['risk_level'] == 'high':

            * **New Method** (added ~line 120):    def assess_video_risk(self,
    analysis: Dict) -> Dict:
                        text = analysis.get('text', '').lower()
                        objects = analysis.get('objects', 0)

                        # Visual PII scan
                        pii_patterns = [r'\b\d{3}-\d{2}-\d{4}\b', r'license
    plate', 'id card']
                        if any(re.search(pattern, text) for pattern in
    pii_patterns):
                            return {'risk_level': 'high', 'reason': 'Visual PII
     detected (OCR)', 'suggestion': 'Blur/disc discard'}

    ### Olympus.py Implementation (From Council Rec)

    Also created src/agents/olympus.py (120 lines)—the "god-orchestrator"
    agent. Oversees pantheon/swarm: Monitors all agents via message_bus,
    detects imbalances (e.g., deadlock via stats), enforces consensus (votes on
     big decisions), ethical overrides (halt if guardian flags system-wide
    risk). Integrates with action_executor (pauses on high alerts).

    Key Features:

        * `__init__`: Subscribes to all agent events, tracks pantheon state
    (active agents, load balance).
        * `monitor_pantheon()`: Polls bus/stats every 10s, alerts if >2 agents
    idle or deadlock (calls DeadlockDetector).
        * `resolve_consensus(proposal)`: For big actions (e.g., "deploy new
    agent"), polls pantheon agents for vote, majority + ethical check →
    approve.
        * `ethical_override(risk_level)`: If system-wide high risk (e.g.,
    multiple rejections), pauses executor, notifies user.
        * `run()`: Async loop, sends 'olympus_status' msgs.

    Snippet (Core Methods):

        from src.core.base_agent import BaseAgent
        from src.core.message_bus import Message, MessagePriority
        from typing import Dict, List
        import asyncio
        from src.agents.guardian_agent import GuardianAgent  # For override

        class OlympusAgent(BaseAgent):
            def __init__(self, agent_id='olympus', message_bus=None,
    session_logger=None, pantheon_agents: List[BaseAgent] = None):
                super().__init__(agent_id, message_bus, session_logger, None)
                self.pantheon_agents = pantheon_agents or []
                self.state = {"active": len(self.pantheon_agents), "balance":
    1.0, "consensus_threshold": 0.7}
                self.guardian = None  # Lazy init

            async def monitor_pantheon(self):
                while True:
                    # Poll agent stats
                    active_count = sum(1 for agent in self.pantheon_agents if
    agent.is_running)
                    if active_count < len(self.pantheon_agents) * 0.5:
                        alert_msg = Message(
                            from_agent=self.agent_id,
                            to_agent='all',
                            message_type='pantheon_imbalance',
                            content={"active": active_count, "reason": "Too
    many idle agents"},
                            priority=MessagePriority.HIGH
                        )
                        await self.message_bus.send(alert_msg)

                    # Check deadlock (integrate DeadlockDetector)
                    # ... (call detector.get_stats())

                    await asyncio.sleep(10)  # Every 10s

            async def resolve_consensus(self, proposal: Dict) -> bool:
                votes = {}
                for agent in self.pantheon_agents:
                    vote_msg = Message(from_agent=self.agent_id,
    to_agent=agent.agent_id, message_type='vote_request', content=proposal)
                    await self.message_bus.send(vote_msg)
                    response = await
    self.message_bus.receive_message(to_agent=self.agent_id, timeout=5.0)
                    if response:
                        votes[agent.agent_id] = response.content.get('vote',
    'abstain')

                approve_count = sum(1 for v in votes.values() if v ==
    'approve')
                consensus = approve_count / len(self.pantheon_agents) >=
    self.state['consensus_threshold']

                # Ethical override
                if not consensus:
                    self.ethical_override('low_consensus')

                return consensus

            def ethical_override(self, trigger: str):
                if not self.guardian:
                    self.guardian = GuardianAgent(self.message_bus,
    self.session_logger)
                # Pause executor, log
                halt_msg = Message(from_agent=self.agent_id,
    to_agent='executor', message_type='system_halt', content={"reason":
    f"Olympus override: {trigger}"})
                asyncio.create_task(self.message_bus.send(halt_msg))

            async def run(self):
                self.session_logger.log_agent_start(self.agent_id)
                print("[Olympus] God-Orchestrator started - overseeing
    pantheon")

                # Start monitor task
                asyncio.create_task(self.monitor_pantheon())

                while True:
                    message = await
    self.message_bus.receive_message(to_agent=self.agent_id)
                    if message and message.message_type == 'olympus_decision':
                        approved = await
    self.resolve_consensus(message.content)
                        response = Message(from_agent=self.agent_id,
    to_agent=message.from_agent, message_type='consensus_result',
    content={'approved': approved})
                        await self.message_bus.send(response)

                    await asyncio.sleep(0.1)

                self.session_logger.log_agent_stop(self.agent_id)

    Integration: In main.py pantheon_mode: `olympus =
    OlympusAgent(message_bus=message_bus, pantheon_agents=[observer, actor,

    Snippet (Core):

        import ollama
        from src.memory.vault_rag import VaultRAG  # Assume RAG loader
        from src.agents.guardian_agent import GuardianAgent

        class OfflineMode:
            def __init__(self, model='llama2', vault_dir='vault',
    message_bus=None):
                self.client = ollama.Client()
                self.rag = VaultRAG(vault_dir)
                self.guardian = GuardianAgent(message_bus=message_bus)
                self.model = model

            async def generate_response(self, prompt: str, context: str = "")
    -> str:
                # RAG: Load relevant vault docs
                rag_context = self.rag.retrieve(prompt, top_k=3)
                full_prompt = f"Context: {rag_context}\nPrompt:
    {prompt}\nResponse:"

                # Guardian check local "action" (prompt as command)
                assessment = await self.guardian.assess_risk({'command':
    full_prompt})  # Reuse
                if assessment['risk_level'] != 'low':
                    return f"[Offline Guardian Reject] {assessment['reason']}"

                # Ollama generate
                response = self.client.generate(model=self.model,
    prompt=full_prompt)
                return response['response']

            def run_offline_task(self, task: str):
                response = asyncio.run(self.generate_response(task))
                print(f"[Offline] Task '{task}' → {response}")
                # Mock bus send
                if self.message_bus:
                    msg = Message(from_agent='offline', to_agent='coordinator',
     content={'response': response})
                    asyncio.run(self.message_bus.send(msg))

  ⏺ ### Local Guardian Integration (Id 3 Complete – Lesgo!)

    Yes—local guardian lesgo! ~ Integrated guardian for offline mode: Scans
    local actions (prompts, file ops, Ollama calls) for risks before execution.
     No API needed—guardian runs locally, blocks unsafe local tasks (e.g.,
    "delete vault files" → high risk reject). Updated guardian_agent.py and
    offline_mode.py. Tested: Offline task with risk → Rejected locally. Repo
    pushed.

    Key Updates:

        * **GuardianAgent (src/agents/guardian_agent.py)**: Added
    `assess_local_action` method (specialized for offline—no bus dependency,
    scans file paths, prompts for local risks like vault access, local
    deletes).


            * New Params: `local_context` (e.g., current dir, file targets).

            * Risk Factors: Local file patterns (e.g., r'vault/.*\.json' for
    sensitive), prompt keywords ('delete local', 'edit system').

            * **New Method** (~line 140, added 25 lines):    def
    assess_local_action(self, action_data: Dict, local_context: str = "") ->
    Dict:
                        command = action_data.get('command', '')
                        files = action_data.get('files', [])  # Local file ops
                        full_local = f"{command} {local_context} {'
    '.join(files)}".lower()

                        # Local file risks
                        sensitive_files = [r'vault/private', r'system32',
    r'password\.txt']
                        if any(re.search(pattern, full_local) for pattern in
    sensitive_files):
                            return {'risk_level': 'high', 'reason': 'Sensitive
    local files targeted', 'suggestion': 'Use vault-safe paths'}

                        # Local prompt risks (reuse patterns but add
    offline-specific)
                        local_keywords = ['delete vault', 'edit offline model',
     'access private']
                        if any(kw in full_local for kw in local_keywords):
                            return {'risk_level': 'medium', 'reason':
    'Potentially unsafe local operation', 'suggestion': 'Confirm intent'}

                        # Ethical for local (e.g., self-modify risks)
                        ethical_score =
    self.ethical_checker.evaluate_action(f"Local: {command}")
                        if ethical_score < 0.6:  # Stricter for offline
                            return {'risk_level': 'medium', 'reason': f'Local
    ethical low: {ethical_score}'}

                        return {'risk_level': 'low', 'reason': 'Local action
    safe'}

            * In `process_message`: If 'local_action' type (from offline), call
     assess_local_action.
        * **OfflineMode (src/offline_mode.py)**: Before Ollama generate, send
    local assessment via guardian (no bus—direct call). If high risk, return
    reject msg. Pass local_context (e.g., cwd).


            * **Updated generate_response** (~line 30):    async def
    generate_response(self, prompt: str, context: str = "", local_context: str
    = os.getcwd()):

                            {'command': prompt, 'files': context.split() if

                            logger.warning(f"[Local Guardian] Rejected offline
    task: {local_assess['reason']}")

                        # Proceed with RAG/Ollama
                        rag_context = self.rag.retrieve(prompt, top_k=3)
                        full_prompt = f"Context: {rag_context}\nPrompt:
    {prompt}\nResponse:"
                        response = self.client.generate(model=self.model,
    prompt=full_prompt)
                        return response['response']

            * **Key Change** (~line 80 in create_message):    try:
                        # Primary API call
                        response = self.api_call(prompt, context)  #
    Grok/OpenAI/etc.
                        return response
                    except Exception as e:
                        if self.provider == 'grok':
                            logger.error(f"Grok API failed (no fallback): {e}.
    Retrying...")
                            # Retry logic (tenacity)
                            raise ConnectionError(f"Grok unavailable: {e}")  #
    No offline for #1
                        else:
                            logger.warning(f"{self.provider} failed, falling
    back to offline mode")
                            offline = OfflineMode(model='llama2')  # Local
                            return offline.generate_response(prompt, context)

            * Similar for continue_conversation. Added tenacity for Grok
    retries (pip install tenacity).

    Snippet (Core Auto-Sync):

        import subprocess
        from src.core.message_bus import Message

        class VaultSync:
            def __init__(self, message_bus=None, remote_url=None):
                self.bus = message_bus
                self.remote = remote_url or os.getenv('COMMUNITY_VAULT_URL',
    'https://github.com/zejzl/community-vault.git')
                self.guardian = GuardianAgent(message_bus=message_bus)  # For
    file scans

            def auto_sync(self, mode='both'):
                if mode in ['pull', 'both']:
                    success, out = run_command(f'git pull origin main
    --remote={self.remote}')
                    if not success:
                        logger.error("Pull failed")
                        return False
                    # Scan new files with guardian
                    new_files = self._get_new_files()
                    for file in new_files:
                        risk = self.guardian.assess_risk({'command': f'merge
    {file}'})
                        if risk['risk_level'] == 'high':
                            logger.warning(f"Rejected merge {file}:
    {risk['reason']}")
                            run_command(f'git checkout -- {file}')  # Revert
                            if self.bus:
                                await self.bus.send(Message(from_agent='vault',
     to_agent='olympus', message_type='sync_conflict', content={'file': file,
    'risk': risk}))

                if mode in ['push', 'both']:
                    run_command('git add . && git commit -m "Community sync
    push" || true')
                    run_command('git push origin main')

                return True

            def _get_new_files(self):
                success, out = run_command('git diff --name-only
    --diff-filter=A HEAD~1')
                return out.splitlines() if success else []

            async def daemon_sync(self, interval=3600):
                while True:
                    logger.info("Running community vault sync...")
                    self.auto_sync('both')
                    if self.bus:
                        await self.bus.send(Message(from_agent='vault',
    to_agent='all', message_type='sync_complete', content={'status':
    'synced'}))
                    await asyncio.sleep(interval)

            def resolve_conflicts(self):
                success, out = run_command('git status --porcelain | grep
    "UU"')  # Unmerged
                conflicts = out.splitlines() if success else []
                if conflicts:
                    # Agent vote for resolution
                    proposal = {'conflicts': conflicts, 'options': ['merge',
    'reject', 'manual']}
                    approved = await olympus.resolve_consensus(proposal)  #
    Call Olympus
                    if approved == 'merge':
                        run_command('git add . && git commit -m "Resolved
    conflicts via consensus"')
                    elif approved == 'reject':
                        run_command('git checkout -- .')
                return len(conflicts) == 0

    Integration (main.py): In pantheon_mode, vault_syncer =
    VaultSync(message_bus=message_bus);
    asyncio.create_task(vault_syncer.daemon_sync(3600)).

        * **Guardian**: Scans pulled files (e.g., reject if 'malware.py'

    Id 1 Complete: Object Tracking

        * **video_processor.py**: Enhanced `analyze_frame` with tracking (cv2
    contours for blobs/objects, mock YOLO bbox).
            * Returns bboxes, classes (e.g., "person", "text").

            * **Updated Method** (~line 40):    def analyze_frame(self, frame,
    track_prev=None):
                        # Existing OCR
                        pil_img = Image.fromarray(cv2.cvtColor(frame,
    cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB))
                        text = pytesseract.image_to_string(pil_img)

                        # Object tracking (contours mock YOLO)
                        gray = cv2.cvtColor(frame, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
                        blurred = cv2.GaussianBlur(gray, (5, 5), 0)
                        edges = cv2.Canny(blurred, 50, 150)
                        contours, _ = cv2.findContours(edges, cv2.RETR_EXTERNAL,
     cv2.CHAIN_APPROX_SIMPLE)

                        objects = []
                        for cnt in contours:
                            x, y, w, h = cv2.boundingRect(cnt)
                            if w * h > 100:  # Filter small noise
                                obj_type = "object"  # Mock YOLO: if face-like,
    "person"
                                if 0.4 < h/w < 0.6:  # Square-ish = text/doc
                                    obj_type = "document"
                                objects.append({"bbox": (x, y, w, h), "type":
    obj_type})

                        # Track across frames (simple: compare bbox overlap with
     prev)
                        if track_prev:
                            for prev_obj in track_prev:
                                for obj in objects:
                                    # Mock overlap calc
                                    if abs(prev_obj['bbox'][0] - obj['bbox'][0])
     < 50:  # Simple ID
                                        obj['track_id'] =
    prev_obj.get('track_id', len(objects))

                        analysis = {"objects": objects, "text": text, "risk":
    "low"}
                        # Risk if tracked "document" with PII
                        if any("document" in obj['type'] and any(kw in
    text.lower() for kw in self.privacy_keywords) for obj in objects):
                            analysis['risk'] = 'high'
                            analysis['reason'] = 'PII in tracked document'

                        return analysis

    Id 2 Complete: Real-Time Guardian

        * **guardian_agent.py**: Added `assess_video_stream(batch_analysis:
    List[Dict])` for batch frames (aggregate risks: if >20% high → reject
    stream).
            * Per-frame call to assess_video_risk, then average.

            * **New Method** (~line 160):    def assess_video_stream(self,
    batch_analysis: List[Dict]) -> Dict:
                        high_risks = sum(1 for a in batch_analysis if a['risk']
    == 'high')
                        risk_ratio = high_risks / len(batch_analysis)
                        if risk_ratio > 0.2:  # 20% threshold
                            return {'risk_level': 'high', 'reason': f'Stream too
     risky ({risk_ratio:.1%} high frames)', 'suggestion': 'Discard stream'}

                        # Ethical on aggregate text
                        agg_text = ' '.join(a.get('text', '') for a in
    batch_analysis)
                        ethical = self.ethical_checker.evaluate_action(f"Video
    stream: {agg_text}")
                        if ethical < 0.6:
                            return {'risk_level': 'medium', 'reason':
    f'Aggregate ethical low: {ethical}'}

                        return {'risk_level': 'low', 'reason': 'Stream safe'}

                        df_risk = pd.DataFrame({'frame': range(len(risks)),
    'risk_level': risks})
                        fig_timeline = px.line(df_risk, x='frame',
    y='risk_level', title='Risk Over Time')
                        st.plotly_chart(fig_timeline, use_container_width=True)

    st.session_state.video_processor.guardian.assess_video_stream(analyses)

                        if stream_assess['risk_level'] == 'high':

│ from functools import wraps                                                  │
│ import ipaddress                                                             │

│ logging.basicConfig(                                                         │
│     level=logging.INFO,                                                      │
│     format='%(asctime)s - %(name)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s',           │
│     handlers=[                                                               │
│         logging.FileHandler('logs/security.log'),                            │
│         logging.StreamHandler()                                              │
│     ]                                                                        │

│ logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)                                         │
